line=49743 |   start = 0 
line=49744 |   end = len(array)-1
line=49745 |   while start<end: 
line=49746 |     temp = array[start] 
line=49747 |     array[start] = array[end] 
line=49748 |     array[end] = temp
line=49749 |     start += 1
line=49750 |     end = end-1
line=49751 |   return array
